def convolve_nonneg(a):
 n=a.size
 if n<=512: return np.convolve(a,a)
 size=1 << (2*n-1 -1).bit_length()
 fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
 c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)
 c=c[:2*n-1]
 return c - min(c,0)
```

Weights:
```
def lag_weights(b, mode, beta, K):
    m=len(b)
    p=np.zeros(m)
    if mode==0: # softmax
        x=np.clip(beta*(b - b.max()), -50,0)
        p=np.exp(x); p/=p.sum()
    elif mode==1: # top K uniform
        if K>=m: p.fill(1/m)
        else: idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]; p[idx]=1/K
    elif mode==2: # top K with smooth weights
        idx=...; vals=b[idx]; temp=max((b.max()-vals.min())*0.5,1e-3*b.max())
        p[idx]=exp((vals-b.max())/temp); normalize
    elif mode==3: # active threshold
        tol=b.max()*0.25? active=b>=M*0.75; if none top; normalize
    return p
```
Maybe top K uniform over sorted includes only exact top; if many equal, partition arbitrary. Good.

    candidates=[]
    Gc = G - np.mean(G)
    scaleE = np.max(np.abs(Gc))
    if scaleE>0:
       for rho in (0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0):
          h=a - rho*(np.mean(a)/scaleE)*Gc? Wait Gc scale. We want step = c*mean(a)/scaleE * Gc. If Gc has max=scale, step*Gc max=c*mean(a). Good. `h=a - (c*mean_a/scaleE)*Gc`
          h=clip; normalize; candidates.append(h)
    md = G - np.dot(a,G)*a
    scaleM=np.max(np.abs(md))
    for lam in (0.05,0.2,0.8,3.0,10.0):
       mdn=md/scaleM
       h=a*np.exp(-lam*mdn)
       h=clip; normalize; candidates.append

Random perturb function:
```
def random_perturb(a, rng, scale=0.1, shift_prob=0.1):
    if rng.random()<shift_prob:
       s=int(rng.integers(1,a.size//2+1))
       a=np.roll(a,s) # or -s
       # maybe reflect? if rng.random()<0.5: a=a[::-1]
    h=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    # multiplicative noise
    noise = rng.normal(0,scale,a.size)
    # optional smooth noise
    h = h * np.exp(noise)
    # local block boost
    nb=int(rng.integers(1,6))
    for _ in range(nb):
       length=int(rng.integers(max(1,a.size//50), max(2,a.size//10)+1))
       start=int(rng.integers(0,a.size-length+1))
       h[start:start+length] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, scale*2))
    return normalize_nonneg(h)
```

- Compute B_target = fft(b_target) (length N).

Algorithm:
```
def deconvolve_to_target(a, b_target, Nfft):
   A=fft(a,Nfft)
   B=fft(b_target,Nfft)
   denom=abs(A)**2 + eps
   ratio=B/denom
   # sqrt complex: re=sqrt(re^2+im^2), theta=atan2/2
   mag=np.sqrt(np.abs(ratio)); phase=np.angle(ratio)/2
   A_new=A*mag*np.exp(1j*phase)
   a_new=ifft(A_new).real
   return normalize_nonneg(a_new)
```

```
def waterfill(b, cap_factor):
   b=np.asarray(b,float)
   cap = np.max(b)*cap_factor
   over=b>cap
   excess=b[over].sum()-cap*over.sum()
   if excess<=0: return b
   under=~over
   if under.sum()>0:
       add=excess/under.sum()
       b[under]+=add
       b[over]=cap
   else: # all over, scale down? sum not preserved if all over impossible? If cap<mean, all over; just uniform? choose cap=max
   return b
```

```
def lag_weights(b, mode, beta=None, K=None, temp_frac=None):
    m=b.size
    p=np.zeros(m)
    M=b.max()
    if mode==0: p.fill(1.0/m)
    elif mode==1:
       k=int(min(K,m)); idx=np.argpartition(b, -k)[-k:] if k<m else arange
       p[idx]=1/k
    elif mode==2:
       x=np.clip(beta*(b-M), -50,0); p=np.exp(x); s=p.sum(); if s>0 p/=s
    elif mode==3:
       tol = temp_frac*M if temp_frac else 0.2*M
       active=b >= M-tol
       if active.sum()==0: p[argmax]=1
       else: p[active]=1; p/=active.sum()
    elif mode==4: # weighted active exponential
       tol=...; vals=b[active]; w=np.exp(-((M-vals)/max((M-vals.min()+1e-30),1e-30)))? Simpler softmax on active.
       w=np.exp(beta_active*(vals-M)); p[active]=w; normalize
```
We can define a list of weight specs in local search.

13. candidate_from_direction(a, dir_eu, dir_mirror)
Maybe we generate directly in local.
```
def euclidean_candidates(a, G):
   d=G - np.mean(G)
   s=np.max(np.abs(d))
   if s<=0: return []
   mean_a=np.mean(a)
   out=[]
   for rho in (0.02,0.08,0.25,0.8,2.5):
      h=a - (rho*mean_a/s)*d
      h=normalize_nonneg(h)
      out.append(h)
   return out
def mirror_candidates(a, G):
   dot=np.dot(a,G)
   d=G - dot*a
   s=np.max(np.abs(d))
   if s<=0: return []
   out=[]
   for lam in (0.05,0.2,0.8,3.0,10.0):
      dnorm=d/s
      h=a*np.exp(-lam*dnorm)
      h=normalize_nonneg(h)
      out.append(h)
   return out
```
Maybe too many; adjust: eu r=(0.05,0.2,0.8), mirror l=(0.2,1,4). 6 candidates per weight. Good.
Need `np.exp(-lam*dnorm)` can underflow if lam large and dnorm negative/positive. Clip exponent to [-50,50].
If h sum zero, skip.

14. deconvolve_to_target(a,b_target)
```
def deconvolve_to_target(a, b_target):
    n=a.size
    size=1 << (2*n-1-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    B=np.fft.rfft(b_target,size)
    denom=np.abs(A)**2
    eps=np.finfo(float).tiny*max(1.0, np.max(denom))
    ratio=B/(denom+eps)
    # complex sqrt
    re=ratio.real; im=ratio.imag
    mag=np.sqrt(re*re+im*im)
    # phase half via real/imag of sqrt: for each, sqrt(re+im i)=sqrt((mag+re)/2)+ i sign(im)*sqrt((mag-re)/2)
    sr_mag = np.sqrt(np.maximum((mag+re)/2,0))
    si_mag = np.sqrt(np.maximum((mag-re)/2,0))
    sign=np.sign(im)
    unit_real=sr_mag
    unit_imag=sign*si_mag
    A_new = A * np.complex128(unit_real + 1j*unit_imag) # but A is rfft complex array; multiplication of A (complex) times complex factor? A already complex, factor maybe complex. For rfft arrays, can use complex.
    h=np.fft.irfft(A_new,size)
    h=h[:n]
    return normalize_nonneg(h)
```
Wait A is rfft of real a, B rfft of real b_target. We want A_new = A * sqrt(B/|A|^2). Here denominator |A|^2 real positive. Factor is sqrt(B_real/B? B is complex). But if B is rfft of real, fine. However A * factor yields rfft of real? We use irfft; should be okay if resulting A has Hermitian symmetry? Rfft arrays lack negative frequencies; multiplication by general complex factor may break Hermitian? Rfft representation assumes conjugate symmetry; multiplying positive frequencies by complex factors that are conjugates at negative frequencies automatically? Rfft stores only positive; for a real sequence, A[-k]=conj(A[k]). If we multiply positive frequencies by a complex factor that is not necessarily with conjugate property implied? Rfft only positive, irfft assumes negative are conjugates of positive. That means the resulting spectrum has negative factor conj(factor), which is what we want if factor at negative is conjugate of positive. Since B/|A|^2 is positive? B is spectrum of real b but not symmetric; at positive freq, factor chosen; at negative, irfft uses conj ratio? If we compute factor only at positive from B at positive, its negative conjugate will be conj(B_pos)/|A_neg|^2 = conj(B_pos)/|A_pos|^2 = conj(factor_pos) if denominator same (|A_neg|=|A_pos|). So okay. Use complex.
Need ensure A_new length = rfft length (size//2+1); factor same. But B is rfft length. Good.
If using rfft, A_real? `np.fft.rfft(a,size)` returns complex. Multiplication by complex factor works. `irfft` expects complex with length size//2+1.
Now `b_target` should sum to1. If not, scale? Deconvolution target should preserve sum b = (sum a)^2? If a normalized sum1, sum b=1. target sum1. Good.

Actually min can only reduce, sum<1 for λ<M, cannot get back to1 by min; need distribute excess to below, potentially exceeding λ, then they become capped, etc. The final is waterfill: some top coordinates set to λ, others = b_i, and if some b_i>λ? no; all b_i>λ set λ, but for b_i<λ maybe increase? The projection with upper bound and sum is not just min; it adds to uncapped coordinates, possibly up to λ, and if all capped at λ then sum = λ*m which must equal1, so λ=1/m. More exactly: choose set S capped at λ, others get b_i + (excess/|S^c|) if that ≤λ. This is what iterative does. Good.
       If add to under makes them >λ, next iteration caps them, excess grows; eventually λ maybe higher. This finds minimal max for sum.
    return b
```
We can implement standard active set:
Given desired max level λ < M. Find λ and complement set? Since we want a specific λ (cap target). Need check feasibility: if λ >= 1/m (mean), feasible. For given λ, we need choose set of capped coordinates such that sum of uncapped original plus addition ≤? Actually all coordinates final ≤λ, sum1. The minimal maximal value for projection to box [-∞,λ] and sum1 is: If λ≥mean, feasible. The projection is: sort b descending. Let cumulative. Find k such that (sum top k - kλ) can be distributed among remaining m-k each up to λ. The final λ' maybe. But for simple heuristic, iterative is fine.
```
def waterfill(b, target_level):
    b=np.array(b,float); total=b.sum()
    if total<=0: return b
    b=b/total
    M=b.max(); mean=1/len(b)
    level = min(max(target_level, mean*1.0001), M)
    for _ in range(50):
       over = b > level
       if not over.any(): break
       over_sum = b[over].sum()
       excess = over_sum - level*over.sum()
       if excess <= 1e-15:
           b[over]=level; break
       under = ~over
       if not under.any():
           level = max(level, total/ b.size)
           b.fill(level)
           break
       # Can we distribute excess to under without exceeding level? capacity = level*under.sum() - b[under].sum()
       capacity = level*under.sum() - b[under].sum()
       if excess <= capacity:
           b[under] += excess/under.sum()
           b[over]=level
           break
       else:
           # increase level to reduce excess/capacity? Actually if not enough capacity, need higher level.
           # Find new level such that excess = capacity? level' solves level'*(over+under?) ... easier increase level by excess/b.size
           level = level + excess/ b.size
           if level >= M: break
    # ensure no >level*1.01? final scale to sum1
    b=np.maximum(b,0); s=b.sum(); if s>0 b/=s
    return b
```
This gives a target with max close to level or higher if infeasible. We can call with level = current_M*(1-δ) or target_cap.
But if level not feasible, returns higher. Good.

Actually if level < mean infeasible, set to mean.

`target_cap_for_score(target_L)` = target_L/(n). Since score=2nM, L=score/2, M=L/n. For target L=0.75. Use `target_M = (target_score/2.0)/n`.

In local search, when current M > target_M*1.05, deconv target with level = min(current_M*0.98, target_M*1.1) maybe. We don't want too aggressive.

16. local_search with modes and deconv:
Pseudo:
```
def local_search(a, state, limit, rng, verbose_tag=""):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    n=a.size
    deadline=time.monotonic()+limit
    best_local=a.copy(); best_score=score_normalized(a)
    update_global(state,a,best_score)
    no_improve=0; iter=0
    # precompute weight specs
    if n<=256:
       Ks=[1, max(2,n//32), max(4,n//16), max(8,n//8)]
    elif n<=1024:
       Ks=[1, max(4,n//64), max(16,n//32), max(32,n//16)]
    else:
       Ks=[1, max(8,n//128), max(32,n//64), max(64,n//32)]
    Ks=unique sorted
    beta_sm=5.0*n
    weight_modes=[]
    for K in Ks: weight_modes.append(('top', K))
    weight_modes.append(('softmax', beta_sm))
    weight_modes.append(('active',0.25))
    # Maybe random order per iteration to avoid cycling? Use rotation index
    wi=0
    perturb_scale=0.05
    deconv_counter=0
    while time < deadline - 1:
        b=convolve_nonneg(a); M=float(b.max()); cur=float(2*n*M)
        if cur < best_local - 1e-12:
            best_local=a.copy(); best_score=cur; update_global(state,best_local,best_score); no_improve=0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
        if no_improve > 30:
            # deconv maybe
            improved=False
            for level_frac in (0.9,0.95,0.98):
                target_level = max(1.0/(2*n-1), M*level_frac)
                if target_level < M*(1-1e-6):
                    bt=waterfill(b, target_level)
                    a2=deconvolve_to_target(a,bt)
                    sc2=score_normalized(a2)
                    if sc2 < cur -1e-12:
                        a=a2; cur=sc2; improved=True; no_improve=0; break
            if improved: continue
            # perturb
            a=random_perturb(a,rng,perturb_scale)
            perturb_scale=min(0.5,perturb_scale*1.2)
            no_improve=0
            continue
        # Normal candidate search
        # choose 3 weight modes, maybe rotate
        selected = weight_modes[wi % len(weight_modes) : ] + weight_modes[: wi%len]  # no lambda
        selected = selected[:3]
        wi += 1
        best_h=a; best_sc=cur
        for spec in selected:
            p = make_weights(...)
            if p is None: continue
            G = 2.0*corr_lag(a,p)
            # if G all close, continue
            for h in euclidean_candidates(a,G): sc=score; update
            for h in mirror_candidates(a,G): sc=score; update
        if best_sc < cur -1e-12:
            a=best_h; no_improve=0; perturb_scale=max(0.02,perturb_scale*0.9)
        else:
            no_improve +=1
            if no_improve %10==0:
                # local random small perturb
                h=random_perturb(a,rng,0.03); sc=score
                if sc<cur: a=h
    final update
    return best_local, best_score
```

Potential time: `score_normalized` for many candidates uses convolve_nonneg. For n large, candidate count 3*(6)=18; conv 18; okay.
We can optimize by early skip candidate if normalization fails.

Issue: `mirror_candidates` with d=G - dot*a; if d negative large, exponent can increase some coordinates enormously. `abs` may be large. Candidate may be poor but line search. Need avoid all h concentrated in one coordinate (score bad). Could check `np.max(h) < 0.5/n * something`? We can skip if max(h) > 5*mean(a) maybe too spiky, unless n small. But spikes at edges might be useful? Edge square can be up to sqrt(M) ~sqrt(0.75/n) ~0.04 for n512, while mean .002, ratio20. So allow ratio up to 50. We can skip if max(h) > 100*mean(a) perhaps.

Let's consider performance of `convolve_nonneg`: For many small n, direct np.convolve. For n=1024, direct 1M ops; 1000 evals=1e9 C maybe 1s? FFT size 2048 maybe faster. Use FFT for n>=256? Let's set threshold 128. Implementation:
```
def convolve_nonneg(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size
    if n < 128:
        c=np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
        need=2*n-1
        size=1 << (need-1).bit_length()
        fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
        c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size, n? no, size)[:need]
    mn=c.min()
    if mn<0: c=c-mn
    return c
```
`irfft` returns size; c[need:] maybe tiny. Clip.
If c has NaN due overflow? a normalized, no.

if n<64:
   out=np.zeros(n)
   for i in range(n):
      out[i]=np.dot(a, p[i:i+n])? p length 2n-1; p[i:i+n]
   return out
```
This O(n^2) but small. Use if n<=128.
For p sum1 and a nonneg, out nonnegative.

But b_k derivative is partner mass; reducing a_i reduces b_k by 2 a_{k-i} δa_i. Total reduction ≈2∑δa_i a_{k-i}. If δa_i = -η/|d| d_i, d_i approx partner - mean, reduction positive. Good.
Mirror update decreases a_i by proportional partner. Good.

What if top lag is edge k=0: G_0=2a0, others 0 (if p only k0). d: i0 high, others negative. Euclidean decreases a0, increases all others; good (edge square). Mirror: dot=2a0^2; d_i=2a0δ_i0 -2a0^2 a_i. For i0:2a0^2; others -2a0^2 a_i (negative). d/scale for i0=1, others small negative; h_i0 decreases, others slightly increase; good.

Top lag near center: G_i=2a_{k-i} within overlap. It decreases coordinates whose symmetric partner high. May also increase coordinates with zero partner (outside overlap), shifting mass to edges; might increase edge peaks but line search protects.

Now, deconv waterfill: Let's ensure `waterfill` returns target with sum1. I'll implement standard via sorting for robustness:
We want minimize max of b' subject to b' ≥? We can choose target cap λ ≤ current M. The projection of b onto {x≤λ, sum=1, x≥0?}. If we also want nonnegative, b≥0. Since b nonnegative and λ≥mean, feasible. The solution is: sort b descending. Find largest k such that average of top k? Actually box upper bound: Lagrangian minimize ||x-b||? not our goal, just waterfill. Simple iterative is fine but may not get exact. Let's implement exact waterfill for upper bound λ? Given a desired maximum level `level`, we want b' with all ≤level, sum1, and as close to b? The minimal L2 projection with upper bound and sum=1 can be found by sorting and waterfill. But for heuristic, iterative enough. Use exact-ish:
```
def waterfill(b, level):
    b=np.array(b,float); m=b.size; total=1.0 (after normalize)
    level=max(level, 1.0/m)
    if level >= b.max(): return b
    # We will cap top coordinates at level and distribute excess to others.
    # Iteratively: cap all >level, distribute to rest. If rest become >level, cap them too.
    # This converges to a set S of capped coords with β = level, rest b_i+α.
    # We can find S by sorting descending.
    order=np.argsort(b)[::-1]
    sorted_b=b[order]
    cum=np.cumsum(sorted_b)
    # We need find k maybe such that after capping k largest to level and adding (cum_k - level*k)/(m-k) to remaining, the max remaining <= level.
    # Condition for top k capped: avg_add = (cum_k - k*level)/(m-k) <= level - sorted_b[k] (the largest uncapped value).
    # Also avg_add could be negative if level>average top k? But level<max, for top k likely positive.
    # Find smallest k? Actually cap as many as necessary. Iterate k from1 to m-1.
    for k in range(1,m):
        excess = cum[k-1] - k*level
        if excess <= 0:
            # capping top k doesn't reduce sum enough? Actually if excess<=0, top k average <=level, no need cap k? break.
            break
        add = excess/(m-k)
        if add <= level - sorted_b[k] - 1e-15:
            # feasible: cap top k, add to rest
            b2=b.copy()
            b2[order[:k]]=level
            if m>k: b2[order[k:]] += add
            return b2
    # if none feasible, use overall mean? level=mean
    b2=np.full(m, 1.0/m)
    return b2
```
But this only adds to all remaining, not limiting them except by condition. It ensures remaining max + add ≤level. Good. If add negative? We only consider excess>0. If condition holds. If no k, level too low (<mean); return uniform. This is exact for upper-bound projection? It assumes top k are the only caps. Since sorted, if adding to remaining causes none exceed level (condition), good. We choose k where feasible. If there are ties, okay. This avoids loops. However if top k capped but among remaining some original values might be >level? No, sorted, remaining ≤ sorted_b[k], condition ensures after add ≤level. Good. For level < max, there is feasible k maybe. Let's test b=[10,1,1]/12, level=1. m=3, mean0.333. k=1 excess=9 add=4.5; remaining max1 +4.5=5.5>1 fail. k=2 excess=11-2=9 add=9; remaining max1+9=10>1 fail. none -> uniform. Indeed to keep max≤1 and sum12 impossible (max possible sum=3), so level infeasible; return uniform (max0.333<level? Actually level1 but uniform sum1? scaled? Wait b normalized sum1 not12; example bad). For normalized sum1, b=[0.9,0.05,0.05], m3 mean0.333, level0.4. k1 excess .5 add .25; rem max .05+.25=.30≤.4 feasible: b'=[.4,.3,.3] max.4 sum1. Good.
This exact waterfill caps top k to `level` and adds uniform to rest. It may produce values >level if condition fail; choose next k. Good. But we want reduce max to level; if level below current but infeasible because level < mean, uniform. For n large mean ~1/(2n-1) < level usually. Good.
This waterfill gives a target b with exact max=level (unless infeasible). Nice.
Use it in deconv. Note it may increase many small lags uniformly; target shape could be weird but okay.

Implementation details: b normalized sum1. If `level >= b.max()` return b. If `level < 1/m` set level=1/m. If `level==1/m` uniform.
```
def waterfill_to_level(b, level):
    b=np.asarray(b,float); m=b.size; total=b.sum()
    if total<=0: return np.ones(m)/m
    b=b/total
    mean=1.0/m
    if not np.isfinite(level) or level < mean: level=mean
    if level >= b.max(): return b
    order=np.argsort(b)[::-1]
    sb=b[order]
    cum=np.cumsum(sb)
    for k in range(1,m):
        excess=cum[k-1] - k*level
        if excess <= 0:
            break
        add=excess/(m-k)
        if add <= level - sb[k]:
            out=b.copy()
            out[order[:k]]=level
            if m>k: out[order[k:]] += add
            out=np.maximum(out,0)
            s=out.sum(); if s>0: out/=s
            return out
    return np.full(m, mean)
```
If k=1 and excess positive, add to rest. If add negative? excess positive ensures add positive.
Condition `add <= level - sb[k]` ensures largest rest after add ≤ level. If sb[k] maybe >>level? But by sorted and level maybe below many; condition may fail until k large. Good.

This target has many coordinates at level and rest equal lower value? Actually rest original + add, so varied but ≤level.

Now, deconvolve to target. The target b has sum1. Good.

Let's think of `make_lag_weights` spec:
```
def make_lag_weights(b, spec):
    m=b.size; M=float(b.max()); p=np.zeros(m)
    typ=spec[0]
    if typ=='top':
       k=int(spec[1]); k=min(max(1,k),m)
       if k==m: p.fill(1.0/m)
       else:
          idx=np.argpartition(b, -k)[-k:]
          p[idx]=1.0/k
    elif typ=='softmax':
       beta=float(spec[1])
       x=beta*(b-M)
       x=np.clip(x,-50,0)
       p=np.exp(x); s=p.sum(); if s>0 p/=s
    elif typ=='active':
       frac=float(spec[1]); tol=frac*M
       active=b >= M - tol
       if active.sum()==0: p[int(np.argmax(b))]=1.0
       else: p[active]=1/active.sum()
    elif typ=='softactive':
       frac=float(spec[1]); beta=float(spec[2]) maybe
    return p
```
For active, if frac=0.3, active close to max. If many (n/2) okay.

def try_deconv(a,b,M,cur_score,n):
    if n>4096: return None
    mean_b=1.0/(2*n-1)
    target_M = TARGET_L/n
    levels=[]
    for frac in (0.90,0.95,0.985):
       lvl=M*frac
       cap_tgt=target_M*1.05
       if cap_tgt < M:
          lvl=min(lvl, cap_tgt)
       if lvl > mean_b*1.001 and lvl < M*(1-1e-7):
          levels.append(lvl)
       else:
          lvl2=M*(1-0.02)
          if lvl2>mean_b and lvl2<M: levels.append(lvl2)
    # unique-ish
    best=None

       bt=waterfill_to_level(b,lvl)

       # blends

Now, block projection escape:
Implement `block_project(a,B)`:
```
def block_project(a,B):
   n=a.size; B=min(B,n)
   L=n//B; rem=n%B
   out=np.empty(n)
   pos=0
   for j in range(B):
      length=L + (1 if j<rem else 0)
      s=a[pos:pos+length].sum()
      out[pos:pos+length]=s/length
      pos+=length
   return normalize_nonneg(out)
```
In local_search when stuck, try B=(16,32,64) projected; if improves accept. This may find smoother. Add to no_improve > 10.

Implement `deconvolve_to_target` with complex sqrt: Since A and B are rfft arrays. We can use `np.sqrt(B/(np.abs(A)**2 + eps) + 0j)`, but if denominator small, ratio huge. However A = FFT of normalized a; A[0]=1, other frequencies maybe small. If A near zero, ratio large; factor large, A_new dominated by sqrt(B). Could be okay. Add eps relative to max denom.
```
denom=np.abs(A)**2
max_denom=denom.max()+1e-300
eps=1e-12*max_denom
ratio=B/(denom+eps)
factor=np.sqrt(ratio + 0j)
```
Complex sqrt of negative real yields imaginary; fine. But `np.sqrt` on complex array. Then `A_new=A*factor`. If factor large, A_new large; irfft may have outliers. We can normalize? deconv target not homogeneous? If A scaled, b scales squared. A[0]=sum a? If a sum1, A[0]=1. Factor[0]=sqrt(B[0]/1)=sqrt(1)=1 because B[0]=sum b=1. So DC preserved. Good.
If B[0] not1 due roundoff, factor adjusts sum. We can set factor[0]=1 explicitly to preserve sum? Good idea:
```
factor[0]=1.0+0j
A_new=A*factor
h=irfft(A_new,size)[:n]
h=normalize_nonneg(h)
```
This keeps sum1 before normalization. Good.
Use `np.complex128` arrays.

`normalize_nonneg`:
```
def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1: a=a.reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0: return np.ones(1)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a,0.0,None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
         a = np.ones_like(a)
         s = float(a.size)
    return a/s
```
posinf to1 may be wrong if overflow, but we avoid.

`convolve_nonneg`:
```
def convolve_nonneg(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([0.0])
    if n <= 128:
        c=np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
        need=2*n-1
        size=1 << (need-1).bit_length()
        fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
        c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)
        c=c[:need]
    mn=float(np.min(c))
    if mn < 0.0:
        c = c - mn
    return c
```
If need-1=0 for n=1, (0).bit_length()=0, 1<<0=1 okay.

`score_normalized`:
```
def score_normalized(a):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    n=a.size
    if n<2: 
       # for n=1, score=2
       return 2.0
    c=convolve_nonneg(a)
    m=float(np.max(c))
    return float(2.0*n*m)
```
Actually for n=1, conv [a0^2], sum1 -> score2*1*1=2; formula works. No need special.

`official_score`:

def official_score(seq):
    try:
        val=evaluate_sequence(seq)
    except Exception:
        return float('inf')
    return float(val)
```

`resize_to_length`:
```
def resize_to_length(a,N):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    N=int(N)
    if N<1: N=1
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(N)/N
    if a.size==N: return normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a.size < N:
       m=a.size
       if m==1: return np.ones(N)/N
       x=np.linspace(0.0, float(m-1), N)
       i0=np.floor(x).astype(np.int64)
       i1=np.minimum(i0+1,m-1)
       frac=x-i0
       out=a[i0]*(1-frac)+a[i1]*frac
       return normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(out,0.0))
    else:
       out=np.zeros(N)
       m=a.size
       for i in range(N):
           lo=(i*m)//N
           hi=((i+1)*m)//N
           if hi>lo: out[i]=a[lo:hi].sum()
       return normalize_nonneg(out)
```

`make_initial`: Need ensure if kind=-1/-2 and resize returns. We'll include many kinds.
```
def make_initial(N, kind, rng, best_seq, provided_seq):
    N=int(max(2,N))
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,N)
    if kind==-1:
       if best_seq is not None: return resize_to_length(best_seq,N)
       kind=8
    if kind==-2:
       if provided_seq is not None: return resize_to_length(provided_seq,N)
       kind=8
    if kind==0:
       return np.ones(N)/N
    if kind==1: # gaussian
       mu=float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.8)); sig=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.35))
       a=np.exp(-0.5*((x-mu)/sig)**2)
       return normalize_nonneg(a)
    if kind==2: # exponential
       alpha=float(rng.choice([-3.0,-1.5,-0.7,0.7,1.5,3.0]))
       a=np.exp(alpha*(x-0.5))
       return normalize_nonneg(a)
    if kind==3: # two blocks random
       L=int(rng.integers(max(1,N//10), N//2))
       p=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.85))
       a=np.zeros(N)
       if L>0: a[:L]=p/L
       if N-L>0: a[L:]= (1-p)/(N-L)
       return normalize_nonneg(a)
    if kind==4: # multiblock
       B=int(rng.integers(3,13))
       lengths=(N//B)*np.ones(B,int) etc. Need allocate random.
```
For multiblock lengths: Use rng.dirichlet to get proportions; lengths at least1.
```
props=rng.dirichlet(np.ones(B))
lengths=np.maximum(1, (N*props).astype(int))
while lengths.sum()<N: lengths[int(rng.integers(B))]+=1
while lengths.sum()>N:
    i=int(np.argmax(lengths)); lengths[i]-=1
masses=rng.dirichlet(np.ones(B)*0.7)
a=np.zeros(N); pos=0
for L,mass in zip(lengths,masses):
   if L>0:
      a[pos:pos+L]=mass/L
      pos+=L
   if pos>=N: break
return normalize_nonneg(a)
```
Need ensure `rng.dirichlet` accepts array. Yes.

`random_perturb`:
```
def random_perturb(a,rng,scale=0.10):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size
    h=a.copy()
    if n>1 and rng.random()<0.15:
       s=int(rng.integers(1, max(2,n//5+1)))
       if rng.random()<0.5: h=np.roll(h,s)
       else: h=np.roll(h,-s)
    mean_a=float(np.mean(h))
    if mean_a<=0: mean_a=1.0/n
    # add baseline noise
    h = h + rng.random(n) * (0.05 * scale * mean_a)
    # multiplicative
    h *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, scale, n))
    # block boosts
    nb=int(rng.integers(1,5))
    for _ in range(nb):
       lo_len=max(1,n//100); hi_len=max(2,n//10)
       if hi_len<=lo_len: hi_len=lo_len+1
       width=int(rng.integers(lo_len, hi_len+1))
       if width>=n: width=n
       start=int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
       h[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 2.0*scale))
    return normalize_nonneg(h)
```
If roll after adding baseline? We roll first.

`make_lag_weights` as above. For 'top' with K maybe >m; handle. `np.argpartition` on b if k=m? avoid. If k=1, works.

`corr_lag` direct vs FFT:
```
def corr_lag(a,p):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); p=np.asarray(p,float)
    n=a.size; m=p.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    if n <= 96:
       out=np.empty(n)
       for i in range(n):
          out[i]=float(np.dot(a,p[i:i+n]))
       return out
    need=n+m-1
    size=1 << (need-1).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fp,size)
    return c[m-1 : m-n-1 : -1]
```
Direct dot for p slice; p length 2n-1, i max n-1, slice i:i+n within 2n-1? For i=n-1, slice n-1:2n-1 length n. yes.

`euclidean_candidates`:
```
def euclidean_candidates(a,G,rhos):
    out=[]
    d=G - np.mean(G)
    scale=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale<=1e-300: return out
    mean_a=float(np.mean(a))
    for rho in rhos:
       h=a - (float(rho)*mean_a/scale)*d
       h=normalize_nonneg(h)
       if h.size==a.size and float(h.sum())>0 and np.max(h) <= 200.0*float(np.mean(h))+1e-300:
           out.append(h)
    return out
```
`mirror_candidates` similar.
```
def mirror_candidates(a,G,lams):
    out=[]
    dot=float(np.dot(a,G))
    d=G - dot*a
    scale=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale<=1e-300: return out
    mean_a=float(np.mean(a))
    for lam in lams:
       exp_arg = -float(lam)*d/scale
       np.clip(exp_arg, -50,50,out=exp_arg)
       h=a*np.exp(exp_arg)
       h=normalize_nonneg(h)
       if ...:
           out.append(h)
    return out
```
If a has zeros and d positive for all, h may all positive where a positive. normalize. Good.

`waterfill_to_level`: as above. Ensure if level is None? Use float.
```
def waterfill_to_level(b, level):
    b=np.asarray(b,float); m=b.size
    total=float(b.sum())
    if total<=0 or m==0: return np.ones(m)/m if m>0 else np.array([0.0])
    b=b/total
    mean=1.0/m
    level=float(level)
    if not np.isfinite(level) or level < mean: level=mean
    M=float(np.max(b))
    if level >= M: return b
    order=np.argsort(b)[::-1]
    sb=b[order]
    cum=np.cumsum(sb)
    for k in range(1,m):
       excess=cum[k-1] - float(k)*level
       if excess <= 0: break
       add=excess/float(m-k)
       if add <= level - sb[k]:
          out=b.copy()
          out[order[:k]]=level
          out[order[k:]] += add
          out=np.maximum(out,0.0)
          s=float(out.sum())
          if s>0: out/=s
          return out
    return np.full(m, mean, dtype=float)
```
If `order[k:]` empty? k<m so not empty. Good.

`deconvolve_to_target`:
```
def deconvolve_to_target(a,b_target):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); b_target=np.asarray(b,float)
    n=a.size
    if n<=1: return a.copy()
    size=1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()? need 2n-1. size=1 << (2*n-1 -1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    B=np.fft.rfft(b_target[:2*n-1],size)
    denom=np.abs(A)**2
    maxdenom=float(denom.max()) if denom.size else 1.0
    eps=1e-12*max(maxdenom,1.0)
    ratio=B/(denom+eps)
    with np.errstate(invalid='ignore', divide='ignore'):
       factor=np.sqrt(ratio.astype(np.complex128))
    factor[0]=1.0+0j
    A_new=A*factor
    h=np.fft.irfft(A_new,size)
    h=h[:n]
    h=np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return normalize_nonneg(h)
```
If size calculation: need=2n-1, need-1=2n-2, bit_length. For n=2 need3, need-1=2 bit_length2? 2 in binary10 length2, 1<<2=4, power >=3 yes. Could use `1 << (need-1).bit_length()`.

`try_deconv`:
```
def try_deconv(a,b,M,cur_score):
    n=a.size
    if n>4096: return None
    mean_b=1.0/(2*n-1)
    target_M=TARGET_L/n
    levels=[]
    for frac in (0.90,0.95,0.985):
       lvl=M*frac
       if target_M < M:
          cap=min(lvl, target_M*1.05)
       else:
          cap=lvl
       if cap > mean_b*1.001 and cap < M*(1-1e-7):
          levels.append(cap)
    # also slight below current
    lvl_s=M*0.99
    if lvl_s > mean_b and lvl_s < M and not any(abs(lvl_s-l)<1e-9 for l in levels): levels.append(lvl_s)
    # remove duplicate approximate
    uniq=[]
    for lvl in levels:
       if not any(abs(lvl-u)<1e-9 for u in uniq): uniq.append(lvl)
    best_a=None; best_sc=cur_score
    for lvl in uniq:
       bt=waterfill_to_level(b,lvl)
       ad=deconvolve_to_target(a,bt)
       if ad is None: continue
       for frac_deconv in (1.0,0.75,0.5):
           h=(1.0-frac_deconv)*a + frac_deconv*ad
           h=normalize_nonneg(h)
           sc=score_normalized(h)
           if sc < best_sc -1e-12:
               best_sc=sc; best_a=h
    if best_a is not None: return best_a,best_sc
    return None
```
The `any(abs(...))` loops small; okay. Could avoid for speed but fine.

`local_search`:
Let's write robust.
```
def local_search(a, state, limit, rng, tag=""):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    n=a.size
    if n<2:
       a=np.array([1.0/3,2.0/3])
       n=2
    best_v=a.copy()
    best_sc=score_normalized(a)
    update_global(state, best_v, best_sc)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(0.0,float(limit))
    # specs
    if n<=128:
       Ks=[1,2,4,8]
    elif n<=512:
       Ks=[1,4,16,64]
    elif n<=2048:
       Ks=[1,8,32,128]
    else:
       Ks=[1,16,64,256]
    Ks=sorted(set(int(min(k,n)) for k in Ks))
    specs=[]
    for K in Ks: specs.append(('top',K))
    specs.append(('softmax',5.0*float(n)))
    specs.append(('active',0.3))
    specs=specs[:5]
    if n<=1024:
       max_specs=3; eu_rhos=(0.05,0.2,0.8); mir_lams=(0.2,1.0,4.0)
    elif n<=2048:
       max_specs=3; eu_rhos=(0.1,0.5); mir_lams=(0.5,2.0)
    else:
       max_specs=2; eu_rhos=(0.1,0.5); mir_lams=(0.5,2.0)
    rot=0
    no_improve=0
    perturb_scale=0.05
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()+0.05 < deadline:
        it+=1
        b=convolve_nonneg(a)
        M=float(np.max(b))
        cur=float(2*n*M)
        if cur < best_sc -1e-12:
            best_sc=cur; best_v=a.copy(); update_global(state,best_v,best_sc); no_improve=0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
        if no_improve > 8:
            # try block projection
            proj_done=False
            for B in (8,16,32,64):
               if B>=n: continue
               h=block_project(a,B)
               sc=score_normalized(h)
               if sc < cur -1e-12:
                   a=h; cur=sc
                   if sc<best_sc: ...
                   proj_done=True; no_improve=0
                   break
            if proj_done:
                continue
        if no_improve > 15 and (no_improve % 5 == 0) and n <= 4096:
            res=try_deconv(a,b,M,cur)
            if res is not None:
               a,cur=res
               if cur < best_sc -1e-12:
                   best_sc=cur; best_v=a.copy(); update_global
                   no_improve=0
               continue
        if no_improve > 30:
            # perturb
            best_p=None; best_psc=cur
            for _ in range(3):
               hp=random_perturb(a,rng,perturb_scale)
               scp=score_normalized(hp)
               if scp < best_psc:
                   best_psc=scp; best_p=hp
            if best_p is not None and best_psc < max(3.0, best_sc*1.3):
               a=best_p; cur=best_psc
               if cur < best_sc*1.0? no if worse but accepted
               perturb_scale=min(1.0, perturb_scale*1.15)
            else:
               a=best_v.copy(); cur=best_sc
               perturb_scale=min(1.0, perturb_scale*1.3)
            no_improve=0
            continue
        # normal candidate
        # if no_improve >0, maybe use more specs? okay
        selected=specs[rot:]+specs[:rot]
        selected=selected[:max_specs]
        rot=(rot+1)%max(1,len(specs))
        best_h=a; best_candidate_sc=cur
        for spec in selected:
            p=make_lag_weights(b,spec)
            if p is None: continue
            G=2.0*corr_lag(a,p)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(G)): continue
            for h in euclidean_candidates(a,G,eu_rhos):
               sc=score_normalized(h)
               if sc < best_candidate_sc -1e-12:
                  best_candidate_sc=sc; best_h=h
            for h in mirror_candidates(a,G,mir_lams):
               sc=score_normalized(h)
               if sc < best_candidate_sc -1e-12:
                  best_candidate_sc=sc; best_h=h
        if best_sc? update if improved:

                a=best_h; cur=best_candidate_sc
                no_improve=0
                perturb_scale=max(0.02, perturb_scale*0.9)

            no_improve+=1

    if best_sc > score? maybe compute final cur from a
    final_sc=score_normalized(a)
    if final_sc < best_sc: best_sc=final_sc; best_v=a.copy()
    update_global(state,best_v,best_sc)
    return best_v,best_sc
```

```
def block_project(a,B):
   n=a.size; B=int(B)
   if B<1: B=1
   if B>=n: return a.copy()
   out=np.zeros(n)
   pos=0
   base_len=n//B; rem=n%B
   for j in range(B):
      L=base_len + (1 if j<rem else 0)
      s=float(a[pos:pos+L].sum())
      out[pos:pos+L]=s/L
      pos+=L
   return normalize_nonneg(out)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time

# Target score/2 used by deconvolution repairs.  The reported target upper
# bound is 1.5027...; this is slightly below it.
TARGET_L = 0.7513


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    """Normalize a 1-D array to sum 1 and make every entry nonnegative."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)
    return a / s


def convolve_nonneg(a):
    """Linear self-convolution, with tiny negative FFT roundoff removed."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 128:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        need = 2 * n - 1
        size = 1 << (need - 1).bit_length()
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)
        c = c[:need]
    mn = float(np.min(c))
    if mn < 0.0:
        c = c - mn
    return c


def score_normalized(a):
    """Score = 2*n*max(conv(a,a)) for a normalized to sum 1."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    c = convolve_nonneg(a)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m)


def official_score(seq):
    """Safely call the external evaluator if present."""
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def update_global(state, a, score):
    """Update the best sequence found so far."""
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return False
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return False
    if score < float(state["score"]) - 1e-12:
        state["score"] = float(score)
        state["seq"] = a.copy()
        print(f"new_best score={state['score']:.12f} n={a.size}", flush=True)
        return True
    return False


def load_provided_sequence():
    """Load the global height_sequence_1 if it exists."""
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        return None
    try:
        a = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a is None or a.size == 0:
        return None
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.reshape(-1)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    if float(a.sum()) <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return normalize_nonneg(a)


def resize_to_length(a, N):
    """Resize a normalized sequence to length N by area-preserving/linear resample."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    N = int(N)
    if N < 1:
        N = 1
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N)
    if a.size == N:
        return normalize_nonneg(a)

    if a.size < N:
        m = a.size
        if m == 1:
            return np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N)
        x = np.linspace(0.0, float(m - 1), N)
        i0 = np.floor(x).astype(np.int64)
        i1 = np.minimum(i0 + 1, m - 1)
        frac = x - i0
        out = a[i0] * (1.0 - frac) + a[i1] * frac
        return normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(out, 0.0))
    else:
        m = a.size
        out = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(N):
            lo = (i * m) // N
            hi = ((i + 1) * m) // N
            if hi > lo:
                out[i] = a[lo:hi].sum()
        return normalize_nonneg(out)


def make_initial(N, kind, rng, best_seq, provided_seq):
    """Create a normalized nonnegative initial sequence of length N."""
    N = int(max(2, N))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N)

    if kind == -1:
        if best_seq is not None:
            return resize_to_length(best_seq, N)
        kind = 8
    if kind == -2:
        if provided_seq is not None:
            return resize_to_length(provided_seq, N)
        kind = 8

    if kind == 0:
        return np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N)

    if kind == 1:
        mu = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
        sig = float(rng.uniform(0.10, 0.35))
        a = np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - mu) / sig) ** 2)
        return normalize_nonneg(a)

    if kind == 2:
        alpha = float(rng.choice([-3.0, -1.5, -0.7, 0.7, 1.5, 3.0]))
        a = np.exp(alpha * (x - 0.5))
        return normalize_nonneg(a)

    if kind == 3:
        # Random adjacent two-block start.
        lo_L = 1
        hi_L = max(2, N // 2 + 1)
        L = int(rng.integers(lo_L, hi_L))
        L = min(max(1, L), N - 1)
        p = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.85))
        a = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        a[:L] = p / float(L)
        a[L:] = (1.0 - p) / float(N - L)
        return normalize_nonneg(a)

    if kind == 4:
        # Random multi-block start.
        B = int(rng.integers(3, 13))
        B = min(B, max(2, N // 2))
        props = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(B))
        lengths = np.maximum(1, (N * props).astype(np.int64))
        while lengths.sum() < N:
            lengths[int(rng.integers(B))] += 1
        while lengths.sum() > N:
            i = int(np.argmax(lengths))
            if lengths[i] > 1:
                lengths[i] -= 1
            else:
                break
        masses = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(B) * 0.7)
        a = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        pos = 0
        for L, mass in zip(lengths, masses):
            L = int(L)
            if L > 0 and pos + L <= N:
                a[pos:pos + L] = float(mass) / float(L)
                pos += L
            elif L > 0:
                a[pos:N] = float(mass) / float(N - pos)
                break
        return normalize_nonneg(a)

    if kind == 5:
        # Beta-family starts.
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(-0.3, 2.5))
        beta = float(rng.uniform(-0.3, 2.5))
        xs = np.clip(x, 1e-6, 1.0 - 1e-6)
        a = xs ** alpha * (1.0 - xs) ** beta
        return normalize_nonneg(a)

    if kind == 6:
        # Exponential of a random polynomial.
        deg = int(rng.integers(2, 6))
        coef = rng.normal(0.0, 0.8, size=deg + 1)
        g = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in range(deg + 1):
            g += float(coef[k]) * (x ** k)
        g -= float(np.max(g))
        return normalize_nonneg(np.exp(0.8 * g))

    if kind == 7:
        # Exponential of a low-frequency trigonometric/polynomial profile.
        basis = np.empty((6, N), dtype=np.float64)
        basis[0] = 1.0
        basis[1] = x
        basis[2] = x - 0.5
        basis[3] = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
        basis[4] = np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
        basis[5] = np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x)
        coef = rng.normal(0.0, 0.8, size=6)
        g = basis.T @ coef
        g -= float(np.max(g))
        return normalize_nonneg(np.exp(0.8 * g))

    if kind == 11:
        # One asymmetric hump.
        mu = float(rng.uniform(0.35, 0.9))
        sig = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.30))
        base = float(rng.uniform(0.10, 0.50))
        a = base + np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - mu) / sig) ** 2)
        return normalize_nonneg(a)

    if kind == 12:
        # Two asymmetric humps.
        mu1 = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.65))
        mu2 = float(rng.uniform(0.35, 0.95))
        sig1 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.30))
        sig2 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.30))
        w1 = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.0))
        w2 = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.0))
        base = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.30))
        a = (base
             + w1 * np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - mu1) / sig1) ** 2)
             + w2 * np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - mu2) / sig2) ** 2))
        return normalize_nonneg(a)

    # Default: smoothed random exponential.
    base = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=N)
    win = max(1, N // 50)
    w = np.ones(win, dtype=np.float64) / float(win)
    base = np.convolve(base, w, mode="same")
    base -= float(np.max(base))
    return normalize_nonneg(np.exp(0.7 * base))


def random_perturb(a, rng, scale=0.10):
    """Random multiplicative/block/small-shift perturbation."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    h = a.copy()

    if n > 1 and rng.random() < 0.15:
        hi = max(2, n // 5 + 1)
        s = int(rng.integers(1, hi))
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            h = np.roll(h, s)
        else:
            h = np.roll(h, -s)

    mean_a = float(np.mean(h))
    if mean_a <= 1e-300:
        mean_a = 1.0 / float(n)

    # A little additive mass so zero coordinates can grow.
    h = h + rng.random(n) * (0.05 * float(scale) * mean_a)

    # Multiplicative lognormal noise.
    h *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(scale), size=n))

    # Local block boosts.
    nb = int(rng.integers(1, 5))
    for _ in range(nb):
        lo_len = max(1, n // 100)
        hi_len = max(2, n // 10)
        if hi_len <= lo_len:
            hi_len = lo_len + 1
        width = int(rng.integers(lo_len, hi_len + 1))
        if width >= n:
            width = n
        start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
        h[start:start + width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 2.0 * float(scale)))

    return normalize_nonneg(h)


def make_lag_weights(b, spec):
    """Build weights over convolution lags from a spec."""
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    m = b.size
    p = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    typ = spec[0]

    if typ == "top":
        k = int(spec[1])
        k = min(max(1, k), m)
        if k == m:
            p.fill(1.0 / float(m))
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(b, -k)[-k:]
            p[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)

    elif typ == "softmax":
        beta = float(spec[1])
        M = float(np.max(b))
        x = beta * (b - M)
        x = np.clip(x, -50.0, 0.0)
        p = np.exp(x)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s > 1e-300:
            p /= s
        else:
            p[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0

    elif typ == "active":
        frac = float(spec[1])
        M = float(np.max(b))
        tol = frac * M
        active = b >= M - tol
        if active.sum() == 0:
            p[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        else:
            p[active] = 1.0 / float(active.sum())

    else:
        p[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0

    return p


def corr_lag(a, p):
    """
    Return R_i = sum_j a_j p_{i+j}, for i=0..n-1.
    This is the unnormalized part of the gradient of weighted convolution.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    if n <= 96:
        out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(n):
            out[i] = float(np.dot(a, p[i:i + n]))
        return out

    need = n + m - 1
    size = 1 << (need - 1).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fp, size)
    return c[m - 1:m - n - 1:-1]


def euclidean_candidates(a, G, rhos):
    """Projected Euclidean subgradient candidates."""
    out = []
    d = G - float(np.mean(G))
    scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-300:
        return out
    mean_a = float(np.mean(a))
    for rho in rhos:
        h = a - (float(rho) * mean_a / scale) * d
        h = normalize_nonneg(h)
        if h.size == a.size:
            mean_h = float(np.mean(h))
            if float(np.max(h)) <= 200.0 * mean_h + 1e-300:
                out.append(h)
    return out


def mirror_candidates(a, G, lams):
    """Mirror/simplex multiplicve-update candidates."""
    out = []
    dot = float(np.dot(a, G))
    d = G - dot * a
    scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-300:
        return out
    for lam in lams:
        exp_arg = -float(lam) * d / scale
        exp_arg = np.clip(exp_arg, -50.0, 50.0)
        h = a * np.exp(exp_arg)
        h = normalize_nonneg(h)
        if h.size == a.size:
            mean_h = float(np.mean(h))
            if float(np.max(h)) <= 200.0 * mean_h + 1e-300:
                out.append(h)
    return out


def block_project(a, B):
    """Project to a piecewise-constant sequence with B blocks."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    B = int(B)
    if B < 1:
        B = 1
    if B >= n:
        return a.copy()
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    base_len = n // B
    rem = n % B
    pos = 0
    for j in range(B):
        L = base_len + (1 if j < rem else 0)
        s = float(a[pos:pos + L].sum())
        out[pos:pos + L] = s / float(L)
        pos += L
    return normalize_nonneg(out)


def waterfill_to_level(b, level):
    """
    Waterfill an b-profile so that sum is 1 and, if feasible, max is level.
    This is a heuristic target for deconvolution repairs.
    """
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    m = b.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    total = float(b.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(total) or total <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
    b = b / total
    mean = 1.0 / float(m)
    level = float(level)
    if not np.isfinite(level) or level < mean:
        level = mean
    M = float(np.max(b))
    if level >= M:
        return b

    order = np.argsort(b)[::-1]
    sb = b[order]
    cum = np.cumsum(sb)

    for k in range(1, m):
        excess = float(cum[k - 1]) - float(k) * level
        if excess <= 0.0:
            break
        add = excess / float(m - k)
        if add <= level - float(sb[k]):
            out = b.copy()
            out[order[:k]] = level
            out[order[k:]] += add
            out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
            s = float(out.sum())
            if s > 0.0:
                out /= s
            return out

    return np.full(m, mean, dtype=np.float64)


def deconvolve_to_target(a, b_target):
    """
    Heuristic autoconvolution deconvolution:
    find a sequence whose convolution is closer to b_target while using the
    phase of the current sequence as a guide.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b_target = np.asarray(b_target, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a.copy()
    if b_target.size < 2 * n - 1:
        b_target = np.pad(b_target, (0, 2 * n - 1 - b_target.size))
    else:
        b_target = b_target[:2 * n - 1]

    size = 1 << (2 * n - 2).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b_target, size)
    denom = np.abs(A) ** 2
    max_denom = float(np.max(denom)) if denom.size else 1.0
    eps = 1e-12 * max(max_denom, 1.0)

    with np.errstate(invalid="ignore", divide="ignore"):
        ratio = B / (denom + eps)
        factor = np.sqrt(ratio.astype(np.complex128))

    factor[0] = 1.0 + 0.0j
    A_new = A * factor
    h = np.fft.irfft(A_new, size)
    h = h[:n]
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return normalize_nonneg(h)


def try_deconv(a, b, M, cur_score):
    """Try target-constraint deconvolution repairs; returns (a, score) or None."""
    n = a.size
    if n > 4096:
        return None

    mean_b = 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1)
    target_M = TARGET_L / float(n)

    levels = []
    for frac in (0.90, 0.95, 0.985):
        lvl = float(M) * float(frac)
        if target_M < float(M):
            cap = min(lvl, target_M * 1.05)
        else:
            cap = lvl
        if cap > mean_b * 1.001 and cap < float(M) * (1.0 - 1e-7):
            levels.append(float(cap))

    lvl_s = float(M) * 0.99
    if lvl_s > mean_b and lvl_s < float(M):
        tmp = lvl_s
        found = False
        for u in levels:
            if abs(u - tmp) < 1e-9:
                found = True
                break
        if not found:
            levels.append(float(lvl_s))

    # Remove approximate duplicates.
    uniq = []
    for lvl in levels:
        found = False
        for u in uniq:
            if abs(u - lvl) < 1e-9:
                found = True
                break
        if not found:
            uniq.append(float(lvl))

    best_a = None
    best_sc = float(cur_score)

    for lvl in uniq:
        bt = waterfill_to_level(b, lvl)
        ad = deconvolve_to_target(a, bt)
        if ad is None:
            continue
        for frac_deconv in (1.0, 0.75, 0.5):
            h = (1.0 - frac_deconv) * a + frac_deconv * ad
            h = normalize_nonneg(h)
            sc = score_normalized(h)
            if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = h

    if best_a is None:
        return None
    return best_a, best_sc


def local_search(a, state, limit, rng, tag=""):
    """
    Local search driver:
    - projected Euclidean and mirror subgradient updates,
    - active-lag weights,
    - occasional block projection,
    - deconvolution repair toward waterfilled target profiles,
    - simulated-escape random perturbations.
    """
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        a = np.array([1.0 / 3.0, 2.0 / 3.0], dtype=np.float64)
        n = 2

    best_v = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_normalized(a)
    update_global(state, best_v, best_sc)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(limit))

    if n <= 128:
        Ks = [1, 2, 4, 8]
    elif n <= 512:
        Ks = [1, 4, 16, 64]
    elif n <= 2048:
        Ks = [1, 8, 32, 128]
    else:
        Ks = [1, 16, 64, 256]

    Ks = sorted(set(int(min(k, n)) for k in Ks))
    specs = []
    for K in Ks:
        specs.append(("top", K))
    specs.append(("softmax", 5.0 * float(n)))
    specs.append(("active", 0.3))
    specs = specs[:5]

    if n <= 1024:
        max_specs = 3
        eu_rhos = (0.02, 0.10, 0.40, 1.0)
        mir_lams = (0.10, 0.50, 2.0)
    elif n <= 2048:
        max_specs = 3
        eu_rhos = (0.05, 0.30, 1.0)
        mir_lams = (0.30, 1.2)
    else:
        max_specs = 2
        eu_rhos = (0.08, 0.40)
        mir_lams = (0.40, 1.6)

    rot = 0
    no_improve = 0
    perturb_scale = 0.05
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() + 0.05 < deadline:
        it += 1

        b = convolve_nonneg(a)
        M = float(np.max(b))
        cur = float(2 * n * M)

        if not np.isfinite(M):
            a = best_v.copy()
            continue

        if cur < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = cur
            best_v = a.copy()
            update_global(state, best_v, best_sc)
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        # Coarse escape: project to a block-constant sequence.
        if no_improve > 8 and (no_improve % 3 == 0):
            improved_block = False
            for B in (8, 16, 32, 64):
                if B >= n:
                    continue
                h = block_project(a, B)
                sc = score_normalized(h)
                if sc < cur - 1e-12:
                    a = h
                    cur = sc
                    if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                        best_sc = sc
                        best_v = a.copy()
                        update_global(state, best_v, best_sc)
                    no_improve = 0
                    improved_block = True
                    break
            if improved_block:
                continue

        # Deconvolution repair toward a waterfilled lower envelope.
        if no_improve > 15 and (no_improve % 5 == 0) and n <= 4096:
            res = try_deconv(a, b, M, cur)
            if res is not None:
                a_decv, sc_decv = res
                a = a_decv
                cur = sc_decv
                if cur < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = cur
                    best_v = a.copy()
                    update_global(state, best_v, best_sc)
                no_improve = 0
                continue

        # Random escape.
        if no_improve > 30:
            best_p = None
            best_psc = cur
            for _ in range(3):
                hp = random_perturb(a, rng, perturb_scale)
                scp = score_normalized(hp)
                if scp < best_psc:
                    best_psc = scp
                    best_p = hp
            if best_p is not None and best_psc < max(3.0, best_sc * 1.3):
                a = best_p
                cur = best_psc
            else:
                a = best_v.copy()
                cur = best_sc
                if cur == best_sc:
                    b = convolve_nonneg(a)
                    M = float(np.max(b))
                    cur = float(2 * n * M)
            perturb_scale = min(1.0, perturb_scale * 1.15)
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        # Normal subgradient/mirror candidates from a rotating set of lag weights.
        selected = specs[rot:] + specs[:rot]
        selected = selected[:max_specs]
        rot = (rot + 1) % max(1, len(specs))

        best_h = a
        best_candidate_sc = cur

        for spec in selected:
            p = make_lag_weights(b, spec)
            G = 2.0 * corr_lag(a, p)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(G)):
                continue

            for h in euclidean_candidates(a, G, eu_rhos):
                sc = score_normalized(h)
                if sc < best_candidate_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_candidate_sc = sc
                    best_h = h

            for h in mirror_candidates(a, G, mir_lams):
                sc = score_normalized(h)
                if sc < best_candidate_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_candidate_sc = sc
                    best_h = h

        if best_candidate_sc < cur - 1e-12:
            a = best_h
            cur = best_candidate_sc
            if cur < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = cur
                best_v = a.copy()
                update_global(state, best_v, best_sc)
            no_improve = 0
            perturb_scale = max(0.02, perturb_scale * 0.9)
        else:
            no_improve += 1

    final_sc = score_normalized(a)
    if final_sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = final_sc
        best_v = a.copy()
    update_global(state, best_v, best_sc)
    return best_v, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for a low-score nonnegative step-function height sequence.

    Uses the provided construction, multi-start macro profiles, and a
    subgradient/mirror local search with waterfilled deconvolution repairs.
    Also progressively upsamples the best sequence to longer lengths, where
    the discrete optimum usually moves closer to the continuous limit.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = start + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 90.0)

    state = {"score": float("inf"), "seq": None}

    provided = load_provided_sequence()
    if provided is not None:
        psc = score_normalized(provided)
        update_global(state, provided, psc)

    # Guaranteed valid fallback.
    fallback = np.array([1.0 / 3.0, 2.0 / 3.0], dtype=np.float64)
    update_global(state, fallback, 16.0 / 9.0)

    if provided is not None:
        print(f"provided n={provided.size} score={state['score']:.12f}", flush=True)

    tasks = []

    # Short native refinement of the provided construction.
    if provided is not None:
        n0 = int(provided.size)
        tasks.append((n0, -2, 55.0))

    # Small/mid exploration to find useful basins.
    small_kinds = [3, 6, 11]
    for kind in small_kinds:
        tasks.append((128, kind, 5.0))

    mid_kinds = [0, 2, 3, 5, 6, 7, 11]
    for kind in mid_kinds:
        tasks.append((256, kind, 7.0))

    base_kinds = [3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 11, 12]
    for kind in base_kinds:
        tasks.append((512, kind, 10.0))

    # Upsampled provided construction to longer lengths.
    tasks.append((1024, -2, 70.0))
    for kind in (11, 8, 6, 4, 3, 0):
        tasks.append((1024, kind, 26.0))

    # Larger lengths: usually the main source of improvement.
    tasks.append((2048, -1, 150.0))
    tasks.append((2048, -2, 95.0))
    for kind in (11, 8, 6, 4):
        tasks.append((2048, kind, 34.0))

    tasks.append((4096, -1, 170.0))
    tasks.append((4096, -2, 80.0))
    for kind in (11, 8, 6):
        tasks.append((4096, kind, 35.0))

    # One very long try if time remains; final polishing can continue longer.
    tasks.append((8192, -1, 120.0))

    for N, kind, base in tasks:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now + 4.0 > hard_deadline:
            break
        limit = min(float(base), hard_deadline - now)
        if limit < 3.0:
            continue

        seq = make_initial(N, kind, rng, state["seq"], provided)
        sc = score_normalized(seq)

        # Skip obviously bad long random starts once a strong result exists.
        if (
            np.isfinite(state["score"])
            and state["score"] < 1.8
            and sc > max(2.2, 1.6 * state["score"])
            and kind not in (-1, -2)
        ):
            continue

        local_search(seq, state, limit, rng, tag=f"N{N}_k{kind}")

    # Progressively upsample the current best and polish at longer lengths.
    same_size_no_gain = 0
    while time.monotonic() + 80.0 < hard_deadline:
        if state["seq"] is None:
            break
        cur_n = int(state["seq"].size)

        if cur_n < 512:
            next_n = 512
        elif cur_n < 1024:
            next_n = 1024
        elif cur_n < 2048:
            next_n = 2048
        elif cur_n < 4096:
            next_n = 4096
        elif cur_n < 8192:
            next_n = 8192
        else:
            next_n = cur_n

        limit = min(120.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 40.0)
        if limit < 30.0:
            break

        seq = resize_to_length(state["seq"], next_n)
        old_score = float(state["score"])
        local_search(seq, state, limit, rng, tag=f"ups{next_n}")

        if next_n == cur_n and state["score"] >= old_score - 1e-9:
            same_size_no_gain += 1
            if same_size_no_gain >= 2:
                break
        else:
            same_size_no_gain = 0

    if state["seq"] is None:
        return [float(1.0 / 3.0), float(2.0 / 3.0)]

    cand_arr = np.asarray(state["seq"], dtype=np.float64)
    # Add a tiny floor so the final answer is strictly positive if desired.
    cand_arr = np.maximum(cand_arr, 1e-15)
    cand_arr = cand_arr / float(cand_arr.sum())
    cand = [float(x) for x in cand_arr]

    try:
        off = official_score(cand)
        print(f"final_official score={off:.12f} n={len(cand)}", flush=True)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return cand
```
